# Tree-level MIL results

Generated by `mil/report.py` from the raw out-of-fold prediction CSVs in `outputs/mil/experiments/`. Every result is labelled with the architecture or approach that produced it. All numbers are cross-validated on development trees only; the sealed groups (folders 10, 15, 29, 30, 32) were never loaded. None of this is field validation.

## Notes that apply to every result

- **Earlier clip-level work, ML-for-Senior reference** (`outputs/reference-ml-for-senior/`): **file-level split, leaky.** Its high recall must not be compared with these numbers.
- **Earlier clip-level work in this repository** (CUDA campaign, grouped-CV and ESP32 models): split by component, folder or recorder, not by file. But those runs trained on folder 30, now known to be the same tree as folder 29 (1.7 m apart, recorded at the same time). **Any of their results on the sealed folder 29 would not be independent.** No such holdout result exists yet.
- **Folder 35 is excluded** (neither healthy nor infested). Correction to handoff finding 6: its high-scoring windows came from a pool of clips taken from infested trees over 96 days (it contains a copy of a folder 1 recording), so they may contain real borer activity. They are not clean evidence of false alarms on healthy trees.
- **Grouping:** same-tree evidence only (positions under 5.0 m apart, or shared recordings), never across labels; see `mil/groups.json`. Duplicate recordings were removed.
- **Recording interval is about 5 minutes,** so a 24-recording window usually covers about 2 hours and a 12-recording window about 1 hour.
- **Primary metrics** (fixed in advance): tree-weighted ROC-AUC and tree-weighted balanced accuracy on stride-1 windows. Intervals are 95% stratified cluster bootstraps over trees (1,000 draws).
- **Improvement rule:** a run improves on B0 (same K) only if, for at least one primary metric, the paired 95% interval is above 0 and the gain exceeds the B0 vs B0-s2 seed difference, with neither primary metric significantly worse (`mil/decide.py`).
- **FINAL is chosen on the same cross-validation trees it is scored on,** so its numbers are optimistic. There is no lockbox; the sealed holdout is not evaluated without the user's explicit decision.

## Data used in cross-validation

- **Healthy:** 5 tree groups, 955 recordings; 5 groups have at least 24 recordings in total, 5 at least 12.
- **Infested:** 16 tree groups, 1522 recordings; 9 groups have at least 24 recordings in total, 15 at least 12.
- **Folds:** `mil/folds.json`; folds without a healthy group: [1, 4, 6, 7, 9].
- **Windows are built per folder** (one device deployment); a folder with fewer than K recordings gives one window of all its recordings.

## Composition decisions

- **A-win-K12:** MIL v1 + the augmentations that passed: noise mixing, EQ tilt
- **A-win-K24:** A-win skipped: 0 of 5 single augmentations passed the improvement rule (none); it runs only when 2-4 pass.
- **FINAL-K12:** encoder: trainable CNN (no pretrained-encoder run passed the improvement rule against B0); classifier: linear (H-mlp did not pass); augmentations: noise mixing, EQ tilt; training windows: contiguous (S-skip did not pass); pooling: gated (T-transformer did not pass, M-heads4 did not pass)
- **FINAL-K24:** encoder: frozen AST (best passing encoder run E-AST-lin); classifier: linear (-mlp did not beat -lin); augmentations: none (pretrained encoder; O6 not run); training windows: contiguous (S-skip did not pass); pooling: gated (T-transformer did not pass, M-heads4 did not pass)

## B0: MIL v1 baseline: trainable 4-block CNN clip encoder (log-mel 64x626) + gated attention + linear classifier

- **Settings that differ from B0:** none; K=24; seed 42; 30 epochs x 64 steps; mixed precision True.
- **Final-epoch training loss by bag type (mean over folds):** healthy 0.0006, infested 0.0006.
- **Time:** 41.0 min for 10 folds; peak GPU memory 4420 MB; waiting for training batches 19 s vs GPU 2137 s.
- **Evaluated on:** 5 healthy and 16 infested trees; stride 1: 1992 windows, stride K: 106 windows.
- **Improvement rule vs B0:** reference.

## B0-K12: MIL v1 baseline: trainable 4-block CNN clip encoder (log-mel 64x626) + gated attention + linear classifier [K=12]
